華潤啤酒三十餘載深耕篤行，每一步
發展皆與時代浪潮同頻共振。面對市
場環境的眾多挑戰與機遇，華潤啤酒
始終主動積極應對、靈活適度調整，
建立了多個發展里程碑。二零二五
年，位於深圳的雪花啤酒總部大廈正
式啟用，不僅為華潤啤酒數十年發展
歷程寫下承前啟後的關鍵篇章，更為
其高質量發展注入新的動能。
本年報以華潤啤酒穩健前行、持續發
展業務為核心概念，運用創新的全景
構圖呈現主題，並以啤酒瓶蓋為設
計主軸，融入雪花總部大廈、啤酒小鎮、大麥倉儲等發展關鍵元
素，詮釋華潤啤酒「馭變求新、穩中有進」的發展理念－主動把握
市場發展趨勢、勇於創新突破，同時堅持築牢發展根基，在穩固
根基的基礎上實現高質量、可持續的發展與提升。展望未來，華
潤啤酒將繼續推進高端化和高質量發展，在複雜多變的酒類市場
中緊抓新機遇、實現新突破，以「踔厲奮發開新局」的姿態，續寫
更多里程碑式的發展篇章。

With CR Beer deeply rooted in the industry with dedicated efforts for
over three decades, every step of its development has resonated with
the pulse of the times. Facing numerous challenges and opportunities
in the market environment, CR Beer has consistently responded
proactively and adapted flexibly, establishing multiple developmental
milestones. In 2025, the official opening of the Snow Beer
Headquarters Building in Shenzhen not only marked a pivotal chapter
bridging the past and future of CR Beer’s decades-long journey, but
also injected new momentum into its high-quality development.
Centred on the core concept of CR Beer’s steady advancement and
continuous business development, this annual report utilises an
innovative panoramic composition to present its theme. Featuring
a beer bottle cap as the central design motif, it incorporates key
developmental elements such as the Snow Beer Headquarters Building,
BREWTOWN, and barley storage facilities. This visual narrative
interprets CR Beer’s development philosophy of “navigating change
and pursuing innovation with steady growth”—proactively grasping
market trends and courageously pursuing innovative breakthroughs,
while persistently consolidating its foundation to achieve high-quality,
sustainable development on solid ground. Looking ahead, CR Beer will
continue to advance premiumisation and high-quality development.
By seizing new opportunities and achieving new breakthroughs in
the complex and volatile alcoholic beverage market, CR Beer will
continue to write more milestone chapters of development with the
determination of “striving forward to open a new chapter”.

                                Major Events


      里 程 碑 事 件 | MILESTONE

      •    華潤啤酒總部遷至深圳雪花科創城，積極
           響應大灣區戰略、把握產業升級機遇。

      •    CR Beer relocated its headquarters to
           the Snow Beer Science Innovation City,
           actively aligning with the Greater Bay Area
           strategy and seizing opportunities for
           industrial upgrades.




          重 大 戰 略 項 目 | MAJOR STRATEGIC INITIATIVES

                                                         •   華潤啤酒召開啤白雙賦能年度總結大會，營造賦能創新
                                                             氛圍，堅定「啤白雙賦能」戰略發展方向和決心。

                                                         •   CR Beer convened the Annual Summary Conference on
                                                             the Dual Empowerment Model for Beer + Baijiu Businesses,
                                                             creating an atmosphere of empowerment and innovation,
                                                             and reaffirming its commitment to the “dual empowerment
                                                             model for beer and baijin businesses” strategy.




                                                         •   華潤啤酒召開「十五五」戰略研討專題會，為高質量謀劃
                                                             「十五五」戰略制定奠定基礎。

                                                         •   CR Beer convened a special seminar on the "15th Five-Year"
                                                             strategy, laying the foundation for the meticulous planning
                                                             and formulation of the "15th Five-Year" strategy.




                                                         •   華潤啤酒與國產大麥產業鏈各方深入合作，逐步構建
                                                             「企業+科研+基地+農戶」的協同模式，探索出國產啤麥
                                                             振興的實踐路徑。

                                                         •   CR Beer forged deep collaborations with various parties
                                                             in the domestic barley industry chain, gradually building
                                                             a collaborative model of "Enterprise + Research + Base +
                                                             Farmers", and exploring a practical path for the revitalisation
                                                             of domestically-grown brewing barley.

    Awards” by the Hong Kong Investor Relations Association (HKIRA), as well as the “Best Overall Investor Relations

       重 大 工 程 建 設 | MAJOR CONSTRUCTION PROJECTS

       •   華潤啤酒旗下武漢、河北、濟南、六安等10家工廠完成光伏併網項目，正式投入運行。

       •   Ten of CR Beer’s factories, including those in Wuhan, Hebei, Jinan, and Lu'an, completed their photovoltaic grid
           connection projects and officially commenced operations.



       •   華潤啤酒旗下昌都搬遷新建年產10萬千升（一期6.3萬千升）啤酒工廠項目、福建年產能80萬千升（二期擴
           建喜力40萬）啤酒工廠項目啟動招標；朝陽搬遷新建年產30萬千升（一期20萬千升）啤酒項目綜合樓及聯
           合車間完成主體驗收；福建新建年產能40萬千升（二期10萬千升)啤酒工廠項目進場施工。

       •   CR Beer launched bidding for the relocation and construction of its Changdu factory with an annual capacity of
           100,000 kilolitres (Phase I: 63,000 kilolitres) and the new Fujian factory project with an annual capacity of 800,000
           kilolitres (Phase II: Heineken expansion of 400,000 kilolitres). The main structure of the comprehensive building
           and joint workshop for the relocated and newly constructed Chaoyang factory with an annual capacity of 300,000
           kilolitres (Phase I: 200,000 kilolitres) completed its inspection. On-site construction also commenced for the new
           Fujian factory with an annual capacity of 400,000 kilolitres (Phase II: 100,000 kilolitres).

                                                with China Resources Enterprise to carry out broader and deeper
                                                cooperation in fields such as Mainland-Hong Kong cross-border
                                                transport, multimodal transport, smart warehousing, intelligent
                                                logistics, and mainland trunk transport.

•   華潤啤酒開展定制代加工業務，開發茶啤、果啤、小麥啤等10
    餘款新酒體，與歪馬、盒馬、胖東來等領先終端達成合作，與
    紹興古越龍山、浙茶集團、甘肅504等企業開展跨界聯名。

•   CR Beer launched a custom contract manufacturing business,
    developing over 10 new products such as tea-infused beer, fruit
    beer, and wheat beer. The Group also partnered with leading
    retail platforms such as Waima Songjiu, Hema, and Pangdonglai,
    and engaged in cross-sector co-branding with enterprises such as
    Shaoxing’s Guyue Longshan, Zhejiang Tea Group, and Gansu 504.

化、融合化」發展主綫，奮力譜寫企業高                  of “intelligentisation, greening, and integration” in the plan, we

白酒行業未來發展預期呈現周期性特徵，                  The future development expectation of the baijiu industry exhibits
行業 集中度中長期將進一步提升，馬太效                 cyclical characteristics. In the medium to long term, industry
應加劇，企業 復甦節奏分化，中小酒企業                 concentration will further improve, the Matthew effect will intensify,
更積極拓展差異化賽道。行業從「量價驅                  the pace of enterprise recovery will diverge, and small and medium-
動」轉向「價值深耕」，為中長期渠道效率                 sized liquor enterprises will more actively expand into differentiated
變革、年輕化轉型等的高質量發展鋪路。                  tracks. The industry is shifting from being “volume and price-driven”

行業短期發展預期還要取决於宏觀經濟和                  to “value deepening”, paving the way for high-quality development
消費等因素而持續調整與結構優化，中長                  such as medium-to-long-term channel efficiency transformation and
期則聚焦價值重構與增量開拓。                      youth-oriented transformation. The industry’s short-term development
                                    expectations will also depend on macroeconomic and consumption

                                    the medium to long term will focus on value reconstruction and

       企業風險管理
       Corporate Risk Management


       管理哲學                                       MANAGEMENT PHILOSOPHY
       董事會致力在企業管治方面達致卓越水                          The Board is committed to achieving excellence in corporate
       平，並深信良好的企業管治是本集團業務                         governance and believes that good corporate governance leads to
       長遠成功之道。本集團重視操守準則、企                         the long-term success of the Group’s business. The Group values its
       業政策及規章，並以此作為本集團企業管                         code of conduct, corporate policies and regulations, which serves as
       治的基礎。在經營業務時注重及致力遵守                         the basis for its corporate governance. Compliance with the national
       國家政策及法律法規，保障本公司利益不                         policies as well as the laws and regulations should be the focus and
       受損害。本集團會持續檢討管治常規，並                         goal in the business operation of the Group to protect the Company’s
       根據最新監管要求不斷作出改善。                            interests. The Group will continuously review its governance practices
                                                  and keep on improving in accordance with the latest regulatory
                                                  requirements.

       董事會深信，風險管理是企業管治中不可                         The Board believes that risk management is an integral part
       或缺的一部分。有效且具效率的企業風                          of corporate governance. Effective and efficient corporate risk
       險管理，不但有助本集團釐定風險承受                          management will facilitate the Group’s business development and
       能力，維持可接受的風險水平，更重要是                         operation by setting the appropriate risk appetite, maintaining
       能主動應對風險，促進業務發展及營運，                         acceptable risk level and more importantly, proactively responding to
       從而維護本集團的信譽及保障股東價值。                         risks. It therefore helps safeguard the Group’s reputation and protect
       透過為本集團業務建立恰當的風險管理程                         shareholder value. Through putting in place proper risk management
       序，並定期進行檢討和更新，本集團致力                         process over the Group’s business and carrying out regular reviews
       防患於未然，減少發生風險的可能性和影                         and updates, the Group strives to reduce the likelihood and the impact

期並不合適。所有董事均須在緊隨其獲得                   that it is not appropriate to specify the term of office of the Directors.

董事會授權                        DELEGATION BY THE BOARD

雖然董事會於任何時間均須肩負指引及監           While the Board retains at all times full responsibility for guiding and
察本公司的責任，但亦可按如下方式將董           monitoring the Company, certain responsibilities of the Board are
事會若干責任下放：                    delegated as follows:

(a)   委員會—董事會成立了不同性質的        (a)   Committees — various committees have been established by the
      委員會執行本公司若干特定職能。              Board to administer certain specified functions of the Company’s
      主要的委員會包括執行委員會、               affairs. The main committees include: the Executive Committee,




                                                                                                                       企 業 管 治 • Corporate Governance
      財務委員會、審核委員會、提名委              the Finance Committee, the Audit Committee, the Nomination
      員會及薪酬委員會。當中列明執行              Committee and the Compensation Committee. The terms of
      委員會、審核委員會、提名委員會              reference for Executive Committee, the Audit Committee, the
      及薪酬委員會各自的職責、功能及              Nomination Committee and the Compensation Committee
      組成的職權範圍均已詳載於企業管              which set out, among other things, the duties, functions and
      治手冊中，亦已上載於本公司的網              composition of these committees are set out in the CG Manual
      站，以供查閱；及                     and available on the Company’s website; and

(b)   總裁—本公司業務的日常管理工作        (b)   President — the day-to-day management of the Company’s
      交由總裁處理，而總裁則須向董事              business is delegated to the President who is accountable to the
      會負責。                         Board.

董事會於二零一八年十一月二十一日採納                      The Board also adopted the “Board Diversity Policy” (the “Diversity
了《 董 事 會 多 元 化 政 策 》
                   （「 多 元 化 政 策 」）      Policy”) on 21 November 2018 and amended it on 24 March 2022. It
並於二零二二年三月二十四日加以修訂，                      aims to set out the approach to achieve diversity at the Board.
該政策旨在促進董事會達到多元化。

多元化政策概要披露如下：                            A summary of the Diversity Policy is disclosed as follows:

(1)   本公司明白並擁護擁有董事會成員                   (1)   The Company recognises and embraces the benefits of having




                                                                                                                     企 業 管 治 • Corporate Governance
      多元化，包括性別、年齡、文化及                         diversity, including diversity on gender, age, cultural and
      教育背景或專業經驗的多元化的裨                         educational background or professional experience at the
      益，以提升本公司的表現。該等裨                         Board in order to enhance the performance of the Company.
      益 包 括 (i) 確 保 在 審 議 及 考 慮 董 事 會         These benefits include (i) ensuring that a range of different
      內提出的議題時，能夠在會議桌上                         perspectives can be brought to the table when reviewing and
      帶 來 一 系 列 不 同 的 觀 點； (ii) 使 本 公         considering issues within the Board; (ii) enabling the Company to
      司能夠在適當時候與其持份者作有                         connect effectively with its stakeholders as appropriate; and (iii)
      效的聯繫；及(iii)支持本公司有關改                     supporting the Company’s commitment to improve its corporate
      善其企業管治實踐的承諾。                            governance practices.

(2)   為達致可持續及均衡的發展，本公                   (2)   With a view to achieving sustainable and balanced development,
      司視董事會成員日益多元化為支持                         the Company sees increasing diversity at the Board as an
      其達到其戰略目標及其可持續發                          essential element in supporting the attainment of its strategic

      事會成員多元化，包括但不限於性                         a number of aspects, including but not limited to gender, age,
      別、年齡、文化及教育背景、專業                         cultural and educational background, professional experience,
      經驗、技能、知識及服務任期。董                         skills, knowledge and length of service. All Board appointments

       風險管理及內部監控                                  RISK MANAGEMENT AND INTERNAL CONTROL

       董事會全面負責建立及維持穩健的風險管                         The Board assumes overall responsibility to establish and maintain

             成 立 的 中 國 華 潤 有 限 公 司（「 中 國 華               Resources Company Limited (“CRC”), a company established
             潤 」）。 本 公 司 註 冊 辦 事 處 及 主 要 營               in the Chinese Mainland. The address of the registered office
             業 地 點 的 地 址 為 香 港 灣 仔 港 灣 道 26              and principal place of business of the Company is Room 2301
             號華潤大廈23樓2301及2310室。                         & 2310, 23/F., China Resources Building, No. 26 Harbour Road,
                                                         Wanchai, Hong Kong.

             幣。

        二. 重大會計政策資訊                                2.    MATERIAL ACCOUNTING POLICY
                                                         INFORMATION
        甲    綜合入賬                                  A     CONSOLIDATION
             本集團之綜合財務報告包括本公司                             The consolidated financial statements of the Group include
             及其全部直接及間接附屬公司之財                             the financial statements of the Company and of all its direct
             務報告。於年內收購或出售之附屬                             and indirect subsidiaries. The results of subsidiaries acquired
             公司之業績乃由其實際收購日期起                             or disposed of during the year are included from the effective
             計或截至出售生效日期止（視情況而                            date of acquisition or up to the effective date of disposal, as
             定）納入計算。所有集團內部之重大                            appropriate. All material intra-group transactions and balances
             交易及結餘已於綜合入賬時對銷。                             have been eliminated on consolidation.

             於二零一零年一月一日之前，增加                             Prior to 1 January 2010, the increases in interests in existing
             現有附屬公司權益與收購附屬公司                             subsidiaries were treated in the same manner as the acquisition
             的處理方法一致，即商譽或優惠承                             of subsidiaries, with goodwill or a bargain purchase gain being
             購增益在適當情況下被確認。就減                             recognised where appropriate. For decreases in interests in

             企業是指一套完整的活動和資產，                             A business is an integrated set of activities and assets which

             於各結算日，為評估是否有跡象顯                             At each balance sheet date, both internal and external sources

             回 數 額 會 予 以 估 計 ， 並（ 如 有 關 ）確               where relevant, an impairment loss is recognised to reduce the
             認減值虧損，以將該項資產撇減至                             asset to its recoverable amount. The recoverable amount is the
             其可收回數額。可收回數額為資產                             higher of an asset’s fair value less costs of disposal and value in
             之公平值減出售成本與使用價值之                             use. When it is not possible to estimate the recoverable amount
             較高者。倘無法估計個別資產之可                             individually, the Group estimates the recoverable amount of the
             收回金額，本集團將估計資產所屬                             cash-generating unit (“CGU”) to which the asset belongs. A

             入基本上能獨立於其他資產或資產                             in the consolidated statement of profit and loss account.

      報告所示資產負債賬面值之間的暫           arising between the tax bases of assets and liabilities and their
      時差異而引致的遞延稅項作全數撥           carrying amounts in the consolidated financial statements, with
      備，只有少數情況例外。遞延稅項           limited exceptions. Deferred taxation assets are recognised to
      資產乃就有可能將未來應課稅溢利           the extent that it is probable that future taxable profits will be
      與可動用之短暫時差抵銷而確認。           available against which the temporary difference can be utilised.
      計算遞延稅項時，會以資產變現或           Deferred taxation is calculated at the tax rates that are expected
      負債清還所屬期間預期適用的稅率           to apply to the period when the asset is realised or the liability is
      計算。                       settled.

      遞延稅項負債是就附屬公司、聯營           Deferred taxation liability is provided on temporary differences

      公司和合營公司投資產生的暫時差           arising on investments in subsidiaries, and associates, and
      額作出撥備，除非本集團可控制暫           interests in joint ventures,except for deferred taxation liability
      時差額的撥回時間且暫時差額在可           where the timing of the reversal of the temporary difference is
      預見將來可能不會撥回。               controlled by the Group and it is probable that the temporary

    額，並與其賬面值進行比較。在折                      based on value in use calculation, with their carrying amounts.
    讓現金流量預測中所採用的折讓率                      The key assumptions and judgements adopted by management
    和五年預測期之後的預期收入增長                      in the relevant discounted cash flow forecasts included discount

    二零二五年十二月三十一日的情況                      estimated the present value of future cash flows of the CGUs

        四. 財務風險管理目標及政策                             4.    FINANCIAL RISK MANAGEMENT OBJECTIVE
                                                         AND POLICIES
             本集團之業務性質令本集團須承受                             The Group’s activities expose it to a variety of financial risks:
             各類財務風險，包括信貸風險、流                             credit risk, liquidity risk, interest rate risk and currency risk. The
             動資金風險、利率風險及貨幣風                              management manages and monitors these exposures to ensure
             險。管理層致力管理及監察該等風                             appropriate measures are implemented on a timely and effective
             險，確保及時採取有效措施。                               manner.

        甲    信貸風險                                  A     CREDIT RISK
             於二零二五年十二月三十一日，本                             As at 31 December 2025, the Group’s credit risk is primarily
             集團之信貸風險主要涉及貿易及其                             attributable to trade and other receivables and bank deposits.
             他應收款項及銀行存款。

             本集團的貿易及其他應收款項之信                             The Group has no significant concentrations of credit risk to
             貸風險不存在過分集中情況，因本                             trade and other receivables as its customer bases are widely
             集團的客戶遍佈中國不同地區。                              spread across diverse geographical locations in China. The Group
             本集團已制定政策，確保掛賬客戶                             has policies in place to ensure that open account customers
             財政健全，擁有良好的信貸記錄。                             are financially viable and with an appropriate credit history.
             為盡量降低信貸風險，審批信貸限                             To minimise its credit risk exposure, credit evaluations are
             額前必先進行信貸評估，並執行其                             performed for the determination and approval of credit limits
             他監察措施，確保對逾期未還債務                             granted and other monitoring procedures are implemented
             採取跟進行動。此外亦對賬齡及收                             to ensure that follow-up actions are taken to recover overdue
             回機會作定期檢討，確保為不能收                             debts. In addition, regular reviews on aging and recoverability
             回的款項，作出充分的減值虧損撥                             are performed to ensure that adequate impairment losses are
             備。集團所面對來自貿易及其他應                             made for irrecoverable amounts. The Group’s exposure to trade
             收款項之信貸風險載於附註二十四。                            and other receivables is set out in Note 24.

             至於銀行存款，本集團已定下程序                             In respect of bank deposits, the Group has procedures and
             及政策，確保交易方之信貸評級良                             policies in place to ensure they are made to counterparties with
             好。                                          good credit rating.

        乙    流動資金風險                                B     LIQUIDITY RISK
             為控制流動資金風險，本集團維持                             The Group manages liquidity risk by maintaining sufficient
             充裕現金，並有足夠的已承諾信貸                             cash and the availability of adequate committed credit facilities
             額度可供使用，為業務資本、潛在                             to fund capital, prospective investment opportunities, debt
             投資機會、債務利息及股息付款等                             servicing obligations and dividend payments. Management also
             提供資金。同時，管理層亦密切監                             closely monitors the Group’s rolling forecast and actual cash
             察本集團的滾動資金預測、實際現                             flows and maturity profiles of financial liabilities.
             金流量以及金融負債的到期情況。

             考慮到本集團的負債比率、過往及                             Taking into account the gearing ratio, historical and expected
             預期未來經營現金流及未動用的可                             future cash flows from operations and unutilised available
             用銀行信用額，管理層預計本集團                             banking facilities of the Group, management expected the
             有足夠資源履行到期時的負債及承                             Group has adequate resources to meet its liabilities and
             擔，並在可預見的將來繼續營運。                             commitment as and when they fall due and to continue in
                                                         operational existence for the foreseeable future.

   白酒現金流產生單位因中國白酒消                      For Baijiu cash generating unit (“Baijiu CGU”), the Baijiu CGU
   費業務面臨整體市場挑戰，於截至                      has recorded losses for the year ended 31 December 2025 due
   二零二五年十二月三十一日止年度                      to the overall market challenges of Baijiu consumer business in
   錄得虧損。經減值評估後，集團管                      the PRC. Based on impairment assessment, management of the

             貿易應收款項之減值評估使用的預                             Impairment assessment on trade receivables is using the
             期虧損率乃基於對每個賬齡類別的                             expected loss rates which are based on credit assessments on
             客戶之信貸評估，並按影響客戶結                             each aging category of customers and adjusted for forward-
             清貿易應收款項能力之前瞻性資料                             looking information affecting the ability of the customers to
             作出調整。                                       settle the trade receivables.

             其他應收款項之減值乃按十二個月                             Impairment on other receivables is measured as either 12-month
             預期信貸虧損或全期預期信貸虧損                             expected credit losses or lifetime expected credit loss, depending
             計量，視乎自初始確認起是否出現                             on whether there has been a significant increase in credit risk
             重大信貸風險增加。                                   since the initial recognition.

             根據管理層進行之評估，本集團於                             Based on the assessments performed by management, the fair
